# Tech Debt Cleanup: Action Pane / TasksPane / AgentChat surface

## Goal

Fix a focused set of concrete tech-debt items recently surfaced in the action-pane refactor. All items have specific file references and bounded scope. NO architectural rewrites; surgical fixes only.

## Working tree state (important)

The working tree contains **substantial uncommitted work** from a recently-completed AgentChat → AgentChatPanel refactor that integrated the chat into the right-side TasksPane. Those changes touch:
- `src/features/tasks/components/TasksPane/TasksPane.tsx`
- `src/shared/components/PaneControlTab.tsx`
- `src/shared/contexts/AgentChatContext.tsx`
- `src/tools/video-editor/components/AgentChat/AgentChat.tsx` (renamed export `AgentChat` → `AgentChatPanel`)
- `src/tools/video-editor/components/AgentChat/index.ts`
- `src/app/App.tsx`
- New test file: `src/shared/contexts/AgentChatContext.test.tsx`

**Do not undo or revert any of this work.** It's intentional and stays. The cleanup items below address debt that EXISTS IN OR AROUND that refactor — fix them in place, building on the current code.

The repo also has earlier WIP from commit `be3c5e21` (m1b-m1c-stores branch): drop-to-generation, local media resolver, generations storage mode migration. Don't touch any of that.

## Concrete items to fix

### 1. `[PanesBoot]` render loop in `src/shared/state/panesStore.ts`

**Symptom:** Runtime console shows `[PanesBoot] PanesStoreBootstrapBoundary render` → `useBootstrapPanesStore render` → `effect fires (bootstrapInput changed identity)` → `bootstrap APPLY set()` → repeat. Endless loop visible in `localhost-1777161431011.log` at the project root.

**Cause:** `useBootstrapPanesStore` has a `useEffect` keyed on `bootstrapInput`. The `bootstrapInput` reference changes identity on every render (likely an inline object literal or a useMemo with unstable deps), so the effect fires constantly, triggering `bootstrapPanesStore(...)` → `set()` → re-render → new identity → repeat.

**Fix:** Stabilize the `bootstrapInput` reference. Either memoize it correctly with primitive deps, or compare by value inside the effect before calling `set()`. After fixing the loop, **remove the debug `console.log` lines** at panesStore.ts:406, 522, 528, 541 (they were diagnostic instrumentation; loop is gone, logs aren't needed).

### 2. Z-index consolidation between TasksPane and GenerationsPane

**Symptom:** TasksPane content is `z-100001` (inline literal in `src/features/tasks/components/TasksPane/TasksPane.tsx`). GenerationsPane is `z-100013` (from `UI_Z_LAYERS` in `src/shared/lib/uiLayers.ts`). When GenerationsPane is open/locked, it covers the bottom of TasksPane, making content there visible-but-unreachable. Just patched superficially by shortening TasksPane's `bottom` when GenerationsPane is open — but the underlying inconsistency remains.

**Fix:**
- Add a `TASKS_PANE_CONTENT` entry to `UI_Z_LAYERS` placed appropriately relative to `GENERATIONS_PANE`, `LIGHTBOX_MODAL`, and `TASKS_PANE_TAB_*`. Document the chosen value with a one-line comment explaining the ordering.
- Replace the inline `zIndex: 100001` in `TasksPane.tsx` with the new `UI_Z_LAYERS` constant.
- Verify the layering still works: TasksPane content above MediaLightbox (per the existing code comment), TasksPane content's stacking with respect to GenerationsPane should match the UX intent (probably TasksPane content above GenerationsPane, so the bottom-strip workaround in TasksPane's `bottom: ...` style can be removed too).

### 3. `AgentChatContext` bridge default = noop violates CLAUDE.md

**Symptom:** `useAgentChatBridge()` returns a default value with `replaceSelectedTimelineClips: noopReplace` when no `VideoEditorProvider` is mounted. This silently swallows attachment-removal attempts when AgentChatPanel renders on routes where the video editor isn't mounted. CLAUDE.md explicitly forbids this pattern: "Required-provider hooks must throw when their context is missing. Do not hide missing providers behind default objects with no-op setters."

**Fix:** This is nuanced because the bridge has a real use case: AgentChatPanel renders globally on all tool routes (`/tools`, `/shots`, `/art`), but `VideoEditorProvider` only mounts on `/tools/video-editor`. So the bridge legitimately HAS no real value on other routes.

Two acceptable resolutions — pick whichever is least intrusive:
- (a) Split `useAgentChatBridge` into two: `useAgentChatBridgeRequired` (throws) and `useAgentChatBridgeOptional` (returns null). Update AgentChatPanel callsites to use the optional variant and gate behavior on whether the bridge is available (e.g., disable attachment X clicks when bridge is null).
- (b) Replace the noop with a real failure mode: e.g., warn in dev when removal is called but no provider is registered, AND still update local UI state so the chip disappears (managing attachment state inside AgentChatPanel as a fallback when no bridge override is registered).

Choose the resolution that requires the fewest callsite changes while satisfying CLAUDE.md's spirit (no silent failures).

### 4. `panesStore` subscription consolidation in TasksPane

**Symptom:** TasksPane currently does ~10 individual `usePanesStore((state) => state.X)` calls. Each one is a separate subscription that triggers an independent re-render. Render budget shows TasksPane at 6/5 (over budget) in the log. My recent edit added 3 more subscriptions (`isGenerationsPaneLocked`, `isGenerationsPaneOpen`, `effectiveGenerationsPaneHeight`) which compound the issue.

**Fix:** Consolidate the panesStore reads in TasksPane into a single `usePanesStore` call that returns a tuple/object via shallow equality (`zustand/shallow`). One subscription, one re-render per actual state change. Pattern reference: see how the OLD AgentChat.tsx used `usePanesStore((state) => ({ ... }), shallow)` — the same pattern can apply here.

### 5. TimelineSync silent rejection logging in `src/shared/hooks/usePollSync.ts:166`

**Symptom:** Log shows `[TimelineSync] poll rejected Object` 15+ times with NO error context. The rejected reason is logged as `Object` (something like `console.warn('...', err)` where err's toString returns `[object Object]`).

**Fix:** Update the log call at usePollSync.ts:166 to print the actual error properties: status code, message, stack if dev. Use `console.error('[TimelineSync] poll rejected', { error: err?.message, status: err?.status, ...err })` or similar. Don't change the polling behavior; only improve diagnosability.

This will help diagnose why generations aren't showing in the UI on the user's current dev session — but the actual fix for "generations not showing" is OUT OF SCOPE for this run (different surface area). We just want better logging here.

## Out of scope

- AgentChatPanel deeply coupling to `@/tools/video-editor/hooks/...` — that's a bigger architectural rework, not in this run.
- `usePaneInteractionLifecycle`'s 300ms gate redesign — broader cross-cutting change.
- Any changes to GenerationsPane internals, generation fetching, or the WIP from commit `be3c5e21`.
- Any changes to test infrastructure or mocks beyond what's required to keep existing tests passing.
- The render budgets exceeded for components OUTSIDE TasksPane (PreviewPanel, TimelineCanvas, SortableRow, etc.) — investigate later, not now.
- Anything related to the AgentChat / AgentChatPanel rename or visual layout (the user has been polishing this directly; do not change visual behavior).

## Validation

After implementation:
- `npx tsc --noEmit` passes.
- `npx eslint <touched files>` passes.
- All existing tests still pass: `npx vitest run`. The 3 pre-existing failures (`GenerationsPane.test.tsx:209`, `dropdown-menu.test.tsx` × 2) from the WIP commit are NOT yours to fix — leave them as-is, they exist before and after this work.
- Add a regression test for item 1 (PanesBoot loop) if practical: assert that bootstrap doesn't fire more than once per actual state change.
- Manually verify the runtime log no longer shows the `[PanesBoot]` spam.

## Notes for the planner

- This codebase uses Vite + React 18 + Zustand + React Router v6 + shadcn UI + Tailwind v3.
- See `CLAUDE.md` at the repo root for project-wide rules. Highly relevant: the "Context hooks" section that motivates item 3.
- The user has confirmed: do not commit anything during execution. Leave changes uncommitted in the working tree for the user to review.
- Be careful with z-index changes (item 2) — they affect cross-component stacking. Ensure mobile/desktop both still work.

        Plan:
        # Implementation Plan: Tech Debt Cleanup — Action Pane / TasksPane / AgentChat

## Overview
Five surgical fixes around the recently-refactored Action pane (TasksPane + embedded AgentChatPanel). Each one has a clear file and line scope; no architectural rewrites.

The working tree already has substantial in-flight work (the AgentChat → AgentChatPanel refactor). All fixes build on that work — nothing in the existing modified files gets reverted. Three pre-existing test failures (`GenerationsPane.test.tsx:209`, `dropdown-menu.test.tsx` × 2) are out of scope and stay broken.

Key invariants preserved:
- AgentChatProvider stays globally mounted; the optional override path is the only place behavior changes.
- The PanesStore singleton-bootstrap contract (one owner drives runtime-backed state) is unchanged — only the equality predicate and diagnostic logs are touched.
- z-index ordering: TasksPane content stays above MediaLightbox (z-100010) and now also explicitly above GenerationsPane (z-100013), removing the bottom-strip workaround.

## Main Phase

### Step 1: Fix `[PanesBoot]` render loop and remove diagnostic logs (`src/shared/state/panesStore.ts`)
**Scope:** Small

The loop is `bootstrap APPLY set()` firing on every render even when nothing actual changed. Root cause: `areBootstrapInputsEqual` (`src/shared/state/panesStore.ts:216-237`) compares the eight setter function identities returned by `usePaneLockPolicyState`. Those setter identities flip whenever any of `usePaneLockPolicyState`'s upstream deps churns (e.g. `savePaneLocks` from `useUserUIState`), so equality always fails → `set()` fires → re-render → repeat. The `useMemo`d `bootstrapInput` itself can't be stabilized further without changing upstream policy.

1. **Drop function-identity comparisons** from `areBootstrapInputsEqual` (`src/shared/state/panesStore.ts:216-237`). Keep only the data-field comparisons: `owner`, `viewportHeight`, the four `locks.*`, and the three `is*PaneOpen` booleans. Functions are not data.
2. **Restructure `bootstrap`** (`src/shared/state/panesStore.ts:400-419`) so the runtime closures are always refreshed (so the latest `setIs*` callbacks are used) but `set()` only fires when data inputs actually changed:
   ```ts
   bootstrap: (input) => {
     const wasEqual = areBootstrapInputsEqual(activeBootstrapSnapshot, input);
     activeBootstrapOwner = input.owner;
     activeBootstrapSnapshot = input;
     updateBootstrapRuntime(input);
     if (wasEqual) return;
     set((state) => buildBootstrappedState(state, input));
   },
   ```
3. **Remove diagnostic `console.log` calls** at lines 402, 406-413, 522-525, 528, 534, 541. The loop is gone, the instrumentation isn't needed.
4. **Add a regression test** in a new `src/shared/state/panesStore.test.ts` (or alongside if a related test file exists). Render `PanesStoreBootstrapBoundary` with a mock `usePaneLockPolicyState` that returns brand-new function identities on every render but identical data; assert that `bootstrap` only triggers a `set()` once. Use a Zustand store subscription counter.

### Step 2: Consolidate z-index for TasksPane content (`src/shared/lib/uiLayers.ts`, `src/features/tasks/components/TasksPane/TasksPane.tsx`)
**Scope:** Small

1. **Add `TASKS_PANE_CONTENT`** to `UI_Z_LAYERS` (`src/shared/lib/uiLayers.ts:1-15`). Place it above `GENERATIONS_PANE` (100013) and `GENERATIONS_DROP_CHIP` (100014) so the TasksPane content is always reachable, but below `LIGHTBOX_MODAL`-clearing tab variants and below the modal floor at 110000. Suggested value: `100016`. One-line comment explains the ordering ("above GenerationsPane so the bottom strip stays clickable; below 110000 modal floor").
2. **Replace `zIndex: 100001`** with `UI_Z_LAYERS.TASKS_PANE_CONTENT` in `src/features/tasks/components/TasksPane/TasksPane.tsx:251`. Import the layers map at the top of the file.
3. **Remove the bottom-strip workaround** (lines 49-53 reading `isGenerationsPaneLocked`/`isGenerationsPaneOpen`/`effectiveGenerationsPaneHeight`, plus the `bottom: ...` ternary at line 247). Set `bottom: 0` unconditionally. The original justification ("GenerationsPane covers TasksPane bottom") is solved by raising z-index instead.
4. **Update the comment block** at lines 48-50 and 244-247 to remove now-stale references.
5. **Update the backdrop comment** at line 183 (`PaneBackdrop zIndex={100000}`) to note the new TasksPane content z. The PaneBackdrop z literal can stay numeric or be promoted to `UI_Z_LAYERS.TASKS_PANE_BACKDROP` if you prefer (optional polish).

### Step 3: AgentChatContext bridge — remove silent noop (`src/shared/contexts/AgentChatContext.tsx`, `src/tools/video-editor/components/AgentChat/AgentChat.tsx`)
**Scope:** Small

Take the minimum-intrusive route per CLAUDE.md "no silent failures": change the type of `replaceSelectedTimelineClips` from `(clips) => void` to `((clips) => void) | null`, and have the AgentChatProvider default supply `null` (instead of `noopReplace`).

1. **Update `AgentChatContextValue`** (`src/shared/contexts/AgentChatContext.tsx:7-11`): `replaceSelectedTimelineClips: ((clips: SelectedMediaClip[]) => void) | null`.
2. **Delete `noopReplace`** at line 47.
3. **Update `defaultValue`** (lines 57-61) so `replaceSelectedTimelineClips: null` when no override is registered. The override path (`VideoEditorProvider` calls `register(...)`) keeps supplying a real function — that part already works.
4. **Update AgentChatPanel callsites** in `src/tools/video-editor/components/AgentChat/AgentChat.tsx`:
   - `handleRemoveAttachment` (lines 264-285): wrap `replaceSelectedTimelineClips(...)` in `if (replaceSelectedTimelineClips)`. Gallery deselection (`deselectGalleryMatches`) still runs unconditionally — chips disappear correctly even with no override.
   - `handleRemoveShot` (lines 287-301): same pattern.
   - Update the dependency arrays (lines 285, 301) to keep referencing `replaceSelectedTimelineClips`.
5. **Update tests** that mock the bridge:
   - `src/tools/video-editor/components/AgentChat/AgentChat.test.tsx:121` — `replaceSelectedTimelineClips: vi.fn()` is fine (still non-null in the mock state).
   - `src/app/providers/AppProviders.test.tsx:91-99` — read does not call `replaceSelectedTimelineClips`, so unaffected.
   - `src/shared/contexts/AgentChatContext.test.tsx` — if any default-bridge assertion exists, update it to expect `null` (none currently — verified by reading the file).
   - `src/tools/video-editor/contexts/VideoEditorProvider.test.tsx:292` — calls `replaceSelectedTimelineClips([...])` after the override is registered, so unaffected.
6. **Note in code comment** above the type definition: "Optional — null until VideoEditorProvider registers an override. Callsites must guard before invoking."

### Step 4: Consolidate panesStore subscriptions in TasksPane (`src/features/tasks/components/TasksPane/TasksPane.tsx`)
**Scope:** Small

Currently 10 separate `usePanesStore` calls (lines 41-53). Fold into a single subscription with `zustand/shallow`.

1. **Replace** the 10 individual `usePanesStore` calls with one shallow-equality call:
   ```ts
   import { shallow } from 'zustand/shallow';
   ...
   const {
     isTasksPaneLocked, setIsTasksPaneLocked,
     tasksPaneWidth, activeTaskId, setActiveTaskId,
     isTasksPaneOpenProgrammatic, setIsTasksPaneOpenProgrammatic,
   } = usePanesStore((state) => ({
     isTasksPaneLocked: state.isTasksPaneLocked,
     setIsTasksPaneLocked: state.setIsTasksPaneLocked,
     tasksPaneWidth: state.tasksPaneWidth,
     activeTaskId: state.activeTaskId,
     setActiveTaskId: state.setActiveTaskId,
     isTasksPaneOpenProgrammatic: state.isTasksPaneOpen,
     setIsTasksPaneOpenProgrammatic: state.setIsTasksPaneOpen,
   }), shallow);
   ```
2. **Drop the GenerationsPane reads** (`isGenerationsPaneLocked`, `isGenerationsPaneOpen`, `effectiveGenerationsPaneHeight`) — they're removed entirely as part of Step 2.
3. The `useRenderBudget('TasksPane', 5)` call stays; budget should now show comfortably under after both Step 4 and Step 2 land.

### Step 5: Surface poll-rejection reason inline (`src/tools/video-editor/hooks/usePollSync.ts`)
**Scope:** Tiny

The 2nd-arg `details` object IS structured (it does include `reason`/`phase`), but Chrome renders it as `▶ Object`. The brief author wants the reason visible without expanding.

1. **Reformat the message** in `logPollRejection` (`src/tools/video-editor/hooks/usePollSync.ts:207-218`) so phase + reason are inlined into the leading string:
   ```ts
   logTimelineSync(`poll rejected (${phase}: ${reason})`, {
     polledConfigVersion: polledData.configVersion,
     currentConfigVersion: configVersionRef.current,
     editSeq: editSeqRef.current,
     savedSeq: savedSeqRef.current,
     pendingOps: getPendingOpsRef().current,
     isSaving: isSavingRef.current,
   });
   ```
   Keep the rest of the structured detail for expandable inspection. No behavior change.
2. Confirm the dev-only gate in `logTimelineSync` (lines 161-167) is unchanged — production builds stay silent.

### Step 6: Validation
**Scope:** Small

Run the cheap, scoped checks first; broader sweeps last.

1. **Type check, scoped to touched files:** `npx tsc --noEmit`.
2. **Lint touched files:** `npx eslint src/shared/state/panesStore.ts src/shared/lib/uiLayers.ts src/features/tasks/components/TasksPane/TasksPane.tsx src/shared/contexts/AgentChatContext.tsx src/tools/video-editor/components/AgentChat/AgentChat.tsx src/tools/video-editor/hooks/usePollSync.ts`.
3. **Run targeted tests:**
   - `npx vitest run src/shared/state/panesStore.test.ts` (new regression test from Step 1).
   - `npx vitest run src/shared/contexts/AgentChatContext.test.tsx`.
   - `npx vitest run src/tools/video-editor/components/AgentChat/AgentChat.test.tsx`.
   - `npx vitest run src/tools/video-editor/contexts/VideoEditorProvider.test.tsx`.
   - `npx vitest run src/app/providers/AppProviders.test.tsx`.
4. **Full suite:** `npx vitest run`. Expect the 3 pre-existing WIP failures (`GenerationsPane.test.tsx:209`, `dropdown-menu.test.tsx` × 2) to remain — leave them.
5. **Manual runtime check (dev only):**
   - Reload the dev server, watch console: no `[PanesBoot]` spam. Toggle pane locks via UI and observe a single `bootstrap APPLY set()` per real change (won't print since logs were removed — instead verify visual state updates).
   - With GenerationsPane locked open, open TasksPane, scroll to bottom, click the "tasks expanded" handle: it must be clickable (z-index fix).
   - Visit `/shots` (a non-video-editor tool route): AgentChatPanel renders; clicking an attachment X on a gallery-only clip removes the chip without crashing or warning. Visit `/tools/video-editor`: timeline-clip removal still works end-to-end.
   - Watch dev console: `[TimelineSync] poll rejected (preflight: own echo)` style messages now show reason inline.
6. **Do not commit** — leave changes uncommitted per user instruction.

## Execution Order
1. Step 1 first — fixing the render loop unblocks reliable manual testing of the rest.
2. Step 2 next — z-index consolidation removes the GenerationsPane reads in TasksPane, simplifying Step 4.
3. Step 4 — consolidate the now-smaller subscription set.
4. Step 3 — bridge change is independent of the above; can land any time.
5. Step 5 — log-message tweak is independent.
6. Step 6 — validate everything together.

## Validation Order
1. tsc + eslint on touched files.
2. Targeted unit tests (panesStore, AgentChatContext, AgentChat, AppProviders, VideoEditorProvider).
3. Full vitest run (verify only the 3 pre-existing failures remain).
4. Manual dev-server runtime checks.

    "The 3 pre-existing test failures (`GenerationsPane.test.tsx:209`, `dropdown-menu.test.tsx` \u00d7 2) from the WIP commit are out of scope and stay broken across this change.",

    "Inline-formatting `phase: reason` into the log message (rather than restructuring `logTimelineSync`'s signature) is sufficient for the brief's diagnostic intent \u2014 the structured details object is preserved for expandable inspection."

    {

      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: `handleassetdrop()` and the external-drop wrapper are not pure builders; they immediately mutate editor state and call `applyedit()` per asset. that means the plan cannot literally reuse those callers for atomic shot-drop creation without first extracting a lower-level helper, or it will violate its own \"single applyedit\" requirement.",

    {
      "id": "DEBT-111",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked the actual commit/history callers around step 18. `commitdata()` emits `beforecommit` and schedules save on every edit, and `usetimelinehistory.onbeforecommit()` pushes undo entries per commit boundary, so a reconcile-after-delete `applyedit` still fragments one user action into multiple history/save operations unless the plan also changes those caller contracts.",

    },

        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the render-gating cleanup plan against remotion's runtime exports in `node_modules/remotion/dist/cjs/cancel-render.js:46-70`. `cancelrender()` stores an error and throws to abort the render; it is not a benign cleanup for an abandoned `delayrender` handle, so step 2's instruction to call `cancelrender(handle)` on unmount is technically wrong and would abort preview or export with an error.",

        "occurrence_count": 1,

        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the hand-built replacement clip against the existing insert path in `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useassetmanagement.ts:201-252,262-271` and serialization in `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/usetimelinecommit.ts:210-216`. the revised `timelineeditor` sketch still bypasses current insertion invariants such as manual-track geometry defaults and explicit `cliporder` updates, so replacement clips on manual visual tracks will not be initialized the same way as other inserted video clips and may serialize in a different per-track order.",

        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the new `pinnedshotgroupsoverride` design is still underspecified against `materializedata()`. `assembletimelinedata()` computes `stablesignature` from the config it receives, so if the executor literally follows step 2.4 and overwrites `pinnedshotgroups` on an already-materialized `timelinedata` before `commitdata()`, the saved config and the cached `stablesignature` will diverge unless the data is rebuilt from the updated config.",

        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: step 5.1 claims `updatepinnedgroupwithchildren` is 'currently imported by useswitchtofinalvideo.ts:357'. verified at useswitchtofinalvideo.ts:71-130 that this function is actually **defined locally** in useswitchtofinalvideo.ts, not imported from pinned-group-projection.ts. the real imports from pinned-group-projection at :5-8 are `buildgroupfromchildren` and `replacegroupchildren`, both of which are already in step 5's delete list. the plan's characterization is factually wrong (delete list shouldn't include updatepinnedgroupwithchildren as a pinned-group-projection export), but the substantive intent still works: once `buildgroupfromchildren`/`replacegroupchildren` are deleted, the local helper won't compile and step 11.2's 'inline soft-tag construction' rewrite removes it. minor wording error, not a blocking issue.",

        Debt guidance:
        - These are known accepted limitations. Do not re-flag them unless the current plan makes them worse, broadens them, or fails to contain them.
        - Prefix every new concern with a subsystem tag followed by a colon, for example `Timeout recovery: retry backoff remains brittle`.
        - When a concern is recurring debt that still needs to be flagged, prefix it with `Recurring debt:` after the subsystem tag, for example `Timeout recovery: Recurring debt: retry backoff remains brittle`.

Read this file first — it contains 5 checks, each with a question and guidance.
For each check, investigate the codebase, then add your findings to the `findings` array for that check.

Each finding needs:
- "detail": what you specifically checked and what you found (at least a full sentence)
- "flagged": true if this describes a difference, risk, or tension — even if you think it's justified. false only if purely informational with no possible downside.
- Every check must end with at least one finding. Never leave a `findings` array empty. If you found no issue, add one detailed `flagged: false` finding explaining what you checked and why it appears clear.

When in doubt, flag it — the gate can accept tradeoffs, but it can't act on findings it never sees.

Good: {"detail": "Checked callers of nthroot_mod in solveset.py line 1205 — passes prime moduli only, consistent with the fix.", "flagged": false}
Good: {"detail": "The fix handles empty tuples but not single-element tuples which need a trailing comma.", "flagged": true}
Bad: {"detail": "No issue found", "flagged": false}  ← too brief, will be rejected
Bad: {"detail": "The hints suggest approach X but the plan uses Y. However Y is consistent with X's intent.", "flagged": false}  ← a different approach than the hints IS a flag. You found a divergence — flag it. The gate decides if it's acceptable.

After filling in checks, add any additional concerns to the `flags` array (e.g., security, performance, dependencies).
Use the standard format (id, concern, category, severity_hint, evidence). This array can be empty.

Workflow: read the file → investigate → read file again → add finding → write file back. Repeat for each check.

        Additional guidelines:
        - Robustness level: standard. Use balanced judgment. Flag significant risks, but do not spend flags on minor polish or executor-obvious boilerplate.
        - Over-engineering: prefer the simplest approach that fully solves the problem.
        - Reuse existing flag IDs when the same concern is still open.
        - `verified_flag_ids`: list flag IDs from prior iterations that the revised plan actually resolves (e.g., if the plan was revised to fix FLAG-001, and you confirm the fix is correct, include "FLAG-001"). Only include flags you've verified — don't guess.

        Idea:
# Tech Debt Cleanup: Action Pane / TasksPane / AgentChat surface

## Goal

Fix a focused set of concrete tech-debt items recently surfaced in the action-pane refactor. All items have specific file references and bounded scope. NO architectural rewrites; surgical fixes only.

## Working tree state (important)

The working tree contains **substantial uncommitted work** from a recently-completed AgentChat → AgentChatPanel refactor that integrated the chat into the right-side TasksPane. Those changes touch:
- `src/features/tasks/components/TasksPane/TasksPane.tsx`
- `src/shared/components/PaneControlTab.tsx`
- `src/shared/contexts/AgentChatContext.tsx`
- `src/tools/video-editor/components/AgentChat/AgentChat.tsx` (renamed export `AgentChat` → `AgentChatPanel`)
- `src/tools/video-editor/components/AgentChat/index.ts`
- `src/app/App.tsx`
- New test file: `src/shared/contexts/AgentChatContext.test.tsx`

**Do not undo or revert any of this work.** It's intentional and stays. The cleanup items below address debt that EXISTS IN OR AROUND that refactor — fix them in place, building on the current code.

The repo also has earlier WIP from commit `be3c5e21` (m1b-m1c-stores branch): drop-to-generation, local media resolver, generations storage mode migration. Don't touch any of that.

## Concrete items to fix

### 1. `[PanesBoot]` render loop in `src/shared/state/panesStore.ts`

**Symptom:** Runtime console shows `[PanesBoot] PanesStoreBootstrapBoundary render` → `useBootstrapPanesStore render` → `effect fires (bootstrapInput changed identity)` → `bootstrap APPLY set()` → repeat. Endless loop visible in `localhost-1777161431011.log` at the project root.

**Cause:** `useBootstrapPanesStore` has a `useEffect` keyed on `bootstrapInput`. The `bootstrapInput` reference changes identity on every render (likely an inline object literal or a useMemo with unstable deps), so the effect fires constantly, triggering `bootstrapPanesStore(...)` → `set()` → re-render → new identity → repeat.

**Fix:** Stabilize the `bootstrapInput` reference. Either memoize it correctly with primitive deps, or compare by value inside the effect before calling `set()`. After fixing the loop, **remove the debug `console.log` lines** at panesStore.ts:406, 522, 528, 541 (they were diagnostic instrumentation; loop is gone, logs aren't needed).

### 2. Z-index consolidation between TasksPane and GenerationsPane

**Symptom:** TasksPane content is `z-100001` (inline literal in `src/features/tasks/components/TasksPane/TasksPane.tsx`). GenerationsPane is `z-100013` (from `UI_Z_LAYERS` in `src/shared/lib/uiLayers.ts`). When GenerationsPane is open/locked, it covers the bottom of TasksPane, making content there visible-but-unreachable. Just patched superficially by shortening TasksPane's `bottom` when GenerationsPane is open — but the underlying inconsistency remains.

**Fix:**
- Add a `TASKS_PANE_CONTENT` entry to `UI_Z_LAYERS` placed appropriately relative to `GENERATIONS_PANE`, `LIGHTBOX_MODAL`, and `TASKS_PANE_TAB_*`. Document the chosen value with a one-line comment explaining the ordering.
- Replace the inline `zIndex: 100001` in `TasksPane.tsx` with the new `UI_Z_LAYERS` constant.
- Verify the layering still works: TasksPane content above MediaLightbox (per the existing code comment), TasksPane content's stacking with respect to GenerationsPane should match the UX intent (probably TasksPane content above GenerationsPane, so the bottom-strip workaround in TasksPane's `bottom: ...` style can be removed too).

### 3. `AgentChatContext` bridge default = noop violates CLAUDE.md

**Symptom:** `useAgentChatBridge()` returns a default value with `replaceSelectedTimelineClips: noopReplace` when no `VideoEditorProvider` is mounted. This silently swallows attachment-removal attempts when AgentChatPanel renders on routes where the video editor isn't mounted. CLAUDE.md explicitly forbids this pattern: "Required-provider hooks must throw when their context is missing. Do not hide missing providers behind default objects with no-op setters."

**Fix:** This is nuanced because the bridge has a real use case: AgentChatPanel renders globally on all tool routes (`/tools`, `/shots`, `/art`), but `VideoEditorProvider` only mounts on `/tools/video-editor`. So the bridge legitimately HAS no real value on other routes.

Two acceptable resolutions — pick whichever is least intrusive:
- (a) Split `useAgentChatBridge` into two: `useAgentChatBridgeRequired` (throws) and `useAgentChatBridgeOptional` (returns null). Update AgentChatPanel callsites to use the optional variant and gate behavior on whether the bridge is available (e.g., disable attachment X clicks when bridge is null).
- (b) Replace the noop with a real failure mode: e.g., warn in dev when removal is called but no provider is registered, AND still update local UI state so the chip disappears (managing attachment state inside AgentChatPanel as a fallback when no bridge override is registered).

Choose the resolution that requires the fewest callsite changes while satisfying CLAUDE.md's spirit (no silent failures).

### 4. `panesStore` subscription consolidation in TasksPane

**Symptom:** TasksPane currently does ~10 individual `usePanesStore((state) => state.X)` calls. Each one is a separate subscription that triggers an independent re-render. Render budget shows TasksPane at 6/5 (over budget) in the log. My recent edit added 3 more subscriptions (`isGenerationsPaneLocked`, `isGenerationsPaneOpen`, `effectiveGenerationsPaneHeight`) which compound the issue.

**Fix:** Consolidate the panesStore reads in TasksPane into a single `usePanesStore` call that returns a tuple/object via shallow equality (`zustand/shallow`). One subscription, one re-render per actual state change. Pattern reference: see how the OLD AgentChat.tsx used `usePanesStore((state) => ({ ... }), shallow)` — the same pattern can apply here.

### 5. TimelineSync silent rejection logging in `src/shared/hooks/usePollSync.ts:166`

**Symptom:** Log shows `[TimelineSync] poll rejected Object` 15+ times with NO error context. The rejected reason is logged as `Object` (something like `console.warn('...', err)` where err's toString returns `[object Object]`).

**Fix:** Update the log call at usePollSync.ts:166 to print the actual error properties: status code, message, stack if dev. Use `console.error('[TimelineSync] poll rejected', { error: err?.message, status: err?.status, ...err })` or similar. Don't change the polling behavior; only improve diagnosability.

This will help diagnose why generations aren't showing in the UI on the user's current dev session — but the actual fix for "generations not showing" is OUT OF SCOPE for this run (different surface area). We just want better logging here.

## Out of scope

- AgentChatPanel deeply coupling to `@/tools/video-editor/hooks/...` — that's a bigger architectural rework, not in this run.
- `usePaneInteractionLifecycle`'s 300ms gate redesign — broader cross-cutting change.
- Any changes to GenerationsPane internals, generation fetching, or the WIP from commit `be3c5e21`.
- Any changes to test infrastructure or mocks beyond what's required to keep existing tests passing.
- The render budgets exceeded for components OUTSIDE TasksPane (PreviewPanel, TimelineCanvas, SortableRow, etc.) — investigate later, not now.
- Anything related to the AgentChat / AgentChatPanel rename or visual layout (the user has been polishing this directly; do not change visual behavior).

## Validation

After implementation:
- `npx tsc --noEmit` passes.
- `npx eslint <touched files>` passes.
- All existing tests still pass: `npx vitest run`. The 3 pre-existing failures (`GenerationsPane.test.tsx:209`, `dropdown-menu.test.tsx` × 2) from the WIP commit are NOT yours to fix — leave them as-is, they exist before and after this work.
- Add a regression test for item 1 (PanesBoot loop) if practical: assert that bootstrap doesn't fire more than once per actual state change.
- Manually verify the runtime log no longer shows the `[PanesBoot]` spam.

## Notes for the planner

- This codebase uses Vite + React 18 + Zustand + React Router v6 + shadcn UI + Tailwind v3.
- See `CLAUDE.md` at the repo root for project-wide rules. Highly relevant: the "Context hooks" section that motivates item 3.
- The user has confirmed: do not commit anything during execution. Leave changes uncommitted in the working tree for the user to review.
- Be careful with z-index changes (item 2) — they affect cross-component stacking. Ensure mobile/desktop both still work.

        # Implementation Plan: Tech Debt Cleanup — Action Pane / TasksPane / AgentChat

## Overview
Five surgical fixes around the recently-refactored Action pane (TasksPane + embedded AgentChatPanel), revised to address gate flags around z-layer ordering, supporting tests, and documentation hygiene.

The working tree already has substantial in-flight work (the AgentChat → AgentChatPanel refactor). All fixes build on that work — nothing in the existing modified files gets reverted. Three pre-existing test failures (`GenerationsPane.test.tsx:209`, `dropdown-menu.test.tsx` × 2) are out of scope and stay broken.

2. `src/shared/lib/uiLayers.ts`
3. `src/shared/lib/uiLayers.test.ts` (extend existing ordering test)
4. `src/features/tasks/components/TasksPane/TasksPane.tsx`
5. `src/shared/contexts/AgentChatContext.tsx`
6. `src/tools/video-editor/components/AgentChat/AgentChat.tsx`
7. `src/tools/video-editor/contexts/VideoEditorProvider.test.tsx` (call-site guard for nullable setter)
8. `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/usePollSync.ts`
9. `src/domains/media-lightbox/components/LightboxShell.tsx` (comment-only refresh of stale z-layer doc)
10. `src/shared/components/ui/overlay/shared.tsx` (comment-only refresh of stale z-layer doc)
11. NEW: `src/shared/state/panesStore.test.ts` (regression test for the bootstrap loop)

NOT touched (verified out of scope, even though they reference `100001`):
- `src/tools/video-editor/components/VideoEditorLightboxOverlay.tsx` and its test — independent badge overlay; its `z-[100001]` is unrelated to TasksPane content layering.
- `src/features/tasks/components/TasksPane/components/TaskItemTooltip.tsx` — Radix-portaled tooltip; its `z-[100001]` was set against the OLD TasksPane number but operates in a portaled stacking context. Behavior unchanged after our z-bump (still above MediaLightbox at 100000-100010 by class), and changing it constitutes UI behavior change which the brief excludes.

Key invariants preserved:
- AgentChatProvider stays globally mounted; the optional override path is the only place behavior changes.
- The PanesStore singleton-bootstrap contract (one owner drives runtime-backed state) is unchanged — only the equality predicate and diagnostic logs are touched.
- z-ordering relative to Lightbox stays correct: TasksPane content above MediaLightbox (existing comment) AND above GenerationsPane (new), AND above the TasksPane control tab (so the pane visually covers the tab when open).

## Main Phase

### Step 1: Fix `[PanesBoot]` render loop and remove diagnostic logs (`src/shared/state/panesStore.ts`)
**Scope:** Small

Root cause: `areBootstrapInputsEqual` (`src/shared/state/panesStore.ts:216-237`) compares the eight setter function identities returned by `usePaneLockPolicyState`. Those setter identities flip whenever `usePaneLockPolicyState`'s upstream deps churn (e.g. `savePaneLocks` from `useUserUIState`), so equality always fails → `set()` fires → re-render → repeat. The `useMemo`d `bootstrapInput` cannot be stabilized further without changing upstream policy.

1. **Drop function-identity comparisons** from `areBootstrapInputsEqual` (`src/shared/state/panesStore.ts:216-237`). Keep only the data-field comparisons: `owner`, `viewportHeight`, four `locks.*`, three `is*PaneOpen` booleans. Functions are not data.
2. **Restructure `bootstrap`** (`src/shared/state/panesStore.ts:400-419`) so the runtime closures are always refreshed (so the latest `setIs*` callbacks are used) but `set()` only fires when data inputs actually changed:
   ```ts
   bootstrap: (input) => {
     const wasEqual = areBootstrapInputsEqual(activeBootstrapSnapshot, input);
     activeBootstrapOwner = input.owner;
     activeBootstrapSnapshot = input;
     updateBootstrapRuntime(input);
     if (wasEqual) return;
     set((state) => buildBootstrappedState(state, input));
   },
   ```
3. **Remove diagnostic `console.log` calls** at lines 402, 406-413, 522-525, 528, 534, 541. The loop is gone, the instrumentation isn't needed.
4. **Add a regression test** at `src/shared/state/panesStore.test.ts`. Drive the store via `bootstrapPanesStore`/`clearPanesStoreBootstrap` directly (no React render needed): call `bootstrap` twice with brand-new function identities but identical data fields and assert that the store's `set()`-triggered subscription fires only once.

### Step 2: Add `TASKS_PANE_CONTENT` z-layer and extend ordering test (`src/shared/lib/uiLayers.ts`, `src/shared/lib/uiLayers.test.ts`)
**Scope:** Small

Per the gate flag: TasksPane content needs to sit ABOVE the existing tab constants (so the pane visually covers the tab when open — which is the correct UX). The plan's earlier wording said "below tab variants"; that was wrong. Pane content above tab is intended.

Final ordering (low → high) for the right-side surfaces:
- `LIGHTBOX_MODAL` (100010)
- `GENERATIONS_PANE_BACKDROP` (100012)
- `GENERATIONS_PANE` (100013)
- `GENERATIONS_DROP_CHIP` (100014)
- `TASKS_PANE_TAB_ABOVE_LIGHTBOX` (100015) — tab when no lightbox is up
- **`TASKS_PANE_CONTENT` (NEW: 100016)** — pane body covers tab + generations + lightbox
- `TOAST_VIEWPORT` (120000)

1. **Add `TASKS_PANE_CONTENT: 100016`** to `UI_Z_LAYERS` (`src/shared/lib/uiLayers.ts`), placed between `TASKS_PANE_TAB_ABOVE_LIGHTBOX` and `TOAST_VIEWPORT`. One-line comment: "Pane body sits above the tab so the pane visually covers the tab handle when open; above GenerationsPane so the bottom strip stays clickable."
2. **Update the existing comment block** in `uiLayers.ts:5-7` to mention `TASKS_PANE_CONTENT` if appropriate.
3. **Extend `uiLayers.test.ts`** (`src/shared/lib/uiLayers.test.ts:5-13`) with the following assertions inside the same `it(...)` block:
   ```ts
   expect(UI_Z_LAYERS.TASKS_PANE_CONTENT).toBeGreaterThan(UI_Z_LAYERS.LIGHTBOX_MODAL);
   expect(UI_Z_LAYERS.TASKS_PANE_CONTENT).toBeGreaterThan(UI_Z_LAYERS.GENERATIONS_PANE);
   expect(UI_Z_LAYERS.TASKS_PANE_CONTENT).toBeGreaterThan(UI_Z_LAYERS.GENERATIONS_DROP_CHIP);
   expect(UI_Z_LAYERS.TASKS_PANE_CONTENT).toBeGreaterThan(UI_Z_LAYERS.TASKS_PANE_TAB_ABOVE_LIGHTBOX);
   expect(UI_Z_LAYERS.TOAST_VIEWPORT).toBeGreaterThan(UI_Z_LAYERS.TASKS_PANE_CONTENT);
   ```

### Step 3: Replace TasksPane inline z-index and remove bottom-strip workaround (`src/features/tasks/components/TasksPane/TasksPane.tsx`)
**Scope:** Small

1. **Add the import** for `UI_Z_LAYERS` from `@/shared/lib/uiLayers`.
2. **Replace `zIndex: 100001`** at `TasksPane.tsx:251` with `zIndex: UI_Z_LAYERS.TASKS_PANE_CONTENT`.
3. **Remove the bottom-strip workaround**: drop the `usePanesStore` reads at lines 49-53 (`isGenerationsPaneLocked`, `isGenerationsPaneOpen`, `effectiveGenerationsPaneHeight`) and the conditional `bottom: ...` ternary at line 247. Set `bottom: 0` unconditionally.
4. **Update stale numeric comments** in this file: lines 48-50 (the GenerationsPane workaround comment, removed entirely), lines 183 ("z-index just below TasksPane (100001)" — bump to "(100016)"), lines 248-251 (the MediaLightbox comment is still correct because pane content is above lightbox; just refresh the numeric reference if cited).

### Step 4: Consolidate panesStore subscriptions in TasksPane (`src/features/tasks/components/TasksPane/TasksPane.tsx`)
**Scope:** Small

Currently 10 individual `usePanesStore` calls. Fold into a single subscription with `zustand/shallow`. After Step 3, only seven of the original ten reads remain.

1. **Replace** the seven remaining individual `usePanesStore` calls (`TasksPane.tsx:41-47`) with one shallow-equality call:
   ```ts
   import { shallow } from 'zustand/shallow';
   ...
   const {
     isTasksPaneLocked, setIsTasksPaneLocked,
     tasksPaneWidth, activeTaskId, setActiveTaskId,
     isTasksPaneOpenProgrammatic, setIsTasksPaneOpenProgrammatic,
   } = usePanesStore((state) => ({
     isTasksPaneLocked: state.isTasksPaneLocked,
     setIsTasksPaneLocked: state.setIsTasksPaneLocked,
     tasksPaneWidth: state.tasksPaneWidth,
     activeTaskId: state.activeTaskId,
     setActiveTaskId: state.setActiveTaskId,
     isTasksPaneOpenProgrammatic: state.isTasksPaneOpen,
     setIsTasksPaneOpenProgrammatic: state.setIsTasksPaneOpen,
   }), shallow);
   ```
2. The `useRenderBudget('TasksPane', 5)` call stays; budget should now sit comfortably under after Steps 3+4 land.

### Step 5: AgentChatContext bridge — eliminate the silent noop (`src/shared/contexts/AgentChatContext.tsx`, `src/tools/video-editor/components/AgentChat/AgentChat.tsx`, `src/tools/video-editor/contexts/VideoEditorProvider.test.tsx`)
**Scope:** Small

Resolution choice (in response to the `issue_hints` flag): pick the **field-level nullable** variant. The brief lists two options ((a) split hooks, (b) warn + local fallback); neither is a perfect fit because the bridge has a *partial* default — `timelineId` and `timelineClips` are legitimately default-able from settings on non-editor routes, but `replaceSelectedTimelineClips` has no meaningful default. Splitting the whole hook (option a) would force AgentChatPanel to lose access to `timelineId` from settings; introducing a local fallback for clips state (option b) duplicates timeline-side state into the panel. Marking the **single** offending field as nullable and guarding callsites is the smallest change that satisfies CLAUDE.md's "no silent no-op setters" rule. Documented inline at the type definition.

1. **Update `AgentChatContextValue`** (`src/shared/contexts/AgentChatContext.tsx:7-11`):
   ```ts
   export type AgentChatContextValue = {
     timelineId: string | null;
     timelineClips: SelectedMediaClip[];
     // null until VideoEditorProvider registers an override. CLAUDE.md disallows
     // silent no-op setters; callsites must guard before invoking.
     replaceSelectedTimelineClips: ((clips: SelectedMediaClip[]) => void) | null;
   };
   ```
2. **Delete `noopReplace`** at line 47.
3. **Update `defaultValue`** (lines 57-61): set `replaceSelectedTimelineClips: null` (the override path supplies a real function via VideoEditorProvider's existing `register(...)` call — unchanged).
4. **Update AgentChatPanel callsites** in `src/tools/video-editor/components/AgentChat/AgentChat.tsx`:
   - `handleRemoveAttachment` (lines 264-285): wrap `replaceSelectedTimelineClips(...)` in `if (replaceSelectedTimelineClips)`. Gallery deselection (`deselectGalleryMatches`) keeps running unconditionally — chips disappear correctly even when no override is registered.
   - `handleRemoveShot` (lines 287-301): same `if` guard.
   - Dependency arrays at lines 285 and 301 keep `replaceSelectedTimelineClips` (the value is now nullable, but identity-comparison still works).
5. **Update `VideoEditorProvider.test.tsx:292`** (per `correctness-2` / `callers` flags). The test calls `agentChatBridge.replaceSelectedTimelineClips([...])` directly. After the type change this is a TS error. Fix by adding a non-null assertion at that single test callsite — at this point in the test the `VideoEditorProvider` has already mounted and registered an override, so the value is provably non-null:
   ```ts
   agentChatBridge.replaceSelectedTimelineClips!([
     { clipId: 'clip-2', ... },
   ]);
   ```
   No production code uses non-null assertions; this is test-only and the precondition (VideoEditorProvider mounted) is local to the test scenario.
6. **Verify other test files don't break**:
   - `src/tools/video-editor/components/AgentChat/AgentChat.test.tsx:121` — supplies `replaceSelectedTimelineClips: vi.fn()` (non-null); compatible with the new type.
   - `src/app/providers/AppProviders.test.tsx:91-99` — only reads `timelineId`/`timelineClips`; unaffected.
   - `src/shared/contexts/AgentChatContext.test.tsx` — has no assertion on the default `replaceSelectedTimelineClips` value (verified by reading the file); unaffected.

### Step 6: Surface poll-rejection reason inline (`src/tools/video-editor/hooks/usePollSync.ts`)
**Scope:** Tiny

The 2nd-arg `details` object IS already structured (it includes `reason`/`phase`), but Chrome renders it as `▶ Object`, hiding the reason. Inline the most diagnostic fields into the leading message.

1. **Reformat the message** in `logPollRejection` (`src/tools/video-editor/hooks/usePollSync.ts:207-218`):
   ```ts
   logTimelineSync(`poll rejected (${phase}: ${reason})`, {
     polledConfigVersion: polledData.configVersion,
     currentConfigVersion: configVersionRef.current,
     editSeq: editSeqRef.current,
     savedSeq: savedSeqRef.current,
     pendingOps: getPendingOpsRef().current,
     isSaving: isSavingRef.current,
   });
   ```
   Behavior unchanged. Production stays silent because of the existing `import.meta.env.DEV` gate at lines 161-167.

### Step 7: Refresh stale z-layer comments in adjacent files (`src/domains/media-lightbox/components/LightboxShell.tsx`, `src/shared/components/ui/overlay/shared.tsx`)
**Scope:** Tiny — comment-only

Per the `scope` flag: two adjacent files contain commentary that documents the cross-surface z-layer contract and references the OLD `100001` literal for TasksPane. After Step 3 those comments will be misleading. Comment-only edits, no behavior change.

1. **`src/domains/media-lightbox/components/LightboxShell.tsx:11`** — update the comment from `at z-[100001]` to reference `UI_Z_LAYERS.TASKS_PANE_CONTENT` (or the numeric `100016` with the layer name) so future readers consult the canonical contract.
2. **`src/shared/components/ui/overlay/shared.tsx:4-5`** — update the comment from `TasksPane at 100001, pane tab at 100014` to reference the current layer-map values (`TASKS_PANE_CONTENT = 100016`, `TASKS_PANE_TAB_ABOVE_LIGHTBOX = 100015`). Note: the comment about "pane tab at 100014" is also stale (tab is at 100015, GenerationsPane drop chip is at 100014); fix that drift too.

### Step 8: Validation
**Scope:** Small

1. **Type check, scoped to touched files:** `npx tsc --noEmit`.
2. **Lint touched files:** `npx eslint src/shared/state/panesStore.ts src/shared/lib/uiLayers.ts src/shared/lib/uiLayers.test.ts src/features/tasks/components/TasksPane/TasksPane.tsx src/shared/contexts/AgentChatContext.tsx src/tools/video-editor/components/AgentChat/AgentChat.tsx src/tools/video-editor/contexts/VideoEditorProvider.test.tsx src/tools/video-editor/hooks/usePollSync.ts src/domains/media-lightbox/components/LightboxShell.tsx src/shared/components/ui/overlay/shared.tsx`.
3. **Run targeted tests:**
   - `npx vitest run src/shared/lib/uiLayers.test.ts`
   - `npx vitest run src/shared/state/panesStore.test.ts` (new regression test)
   - `npx vitest run src/shared/contexts/AgentChatContext.test.tsx`
   - `npx vitest run src/tools/video-editor/components/AgentChat/AgentChat.test.tsx`
   - `npx vitest run src/tools/video-editor/contexts/VideoEditorProvider.test.tsx`
   - `npx vitest run src/app/providers/AppProviders.test.tsx`
4. **Full suite:** `npx vitest run`. Expect the 3 pre-existing WIP failures (`GenerationsPane.test.tsx:209`, `dropdown-menu.test.tsx` × 2) to remain — leave them.
5. **Manual runtime check (dev only):**
   - Reload dev server, confirm console no longer shows `[PanesBoot]` spam.
   - With GenerationsPane locked open, open TasksPane, scroll to bottom, click the "tasks expanded" handle: it must be clickable.
   - With TasksPane open, confirm the right-side tab is visually covered by the pane edge (tab z-index is below pane content).
   - Visit `/shots`: AgentChatPanel renders; clicking an attachment X on a gallery-only clip removes the chip without throwing.
   - Visit `/tools/video-editor`: timeline-clip removal still works end-to-end.
   - Watch dev console: `[TimelineSync] poll rejected (preflight: own echo)` style messages now show reason inline.
6. **Do not commit** — leave changes uncommitted per the user's explicit instruction.

## Execution Order
1. Step 1 first — fixing the render loop unblocks reliable manual testing.
2. Step 2 next — define and assert the new z-layer constant before consumers reference it.
3. Step 3 — switch TasksPane to the constant and drop the bottom-strip workaround (drops the GenerationsPane reads, simplifying Step 4).
4. Step 4 — consolidate the now-smaller subscription set.
5. Step 5 — bridge type change + AgentChat callsite guards + VideoEditorProvider test patch (a single coupled change).
6. Step 6 — log-message tweak (independent).
7. Step 7 — comment refresh (last, after the layer values are final).
8. Step 8 — validate.

## Validation Order
1. `tsc` and `eslint` on touched files.
2. Targeted unit tests, starting with `uiLayers.test.ts` and `panesStore.test.ts` (new contracts).
3. Component tests (AgentChatContext, AgentChat, AppProviders, VideoEditorProvider).
4. Full vitest run, verifying only the 3 pre-existing failures remain.
5. Manual dev-server runtime checks.

  "find-the-callers-of-the-changed-function-what-arguments-do-they-actually-pass-does-the-fix-handle-all-of-them": [
    {
      "id": "DEBT-038",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked the current callers of `isrecord` from `llm/messages.ts`. `index.ts` uses it to validate `rawsession`, `db.ts` uses it on db rows, `tool-calls.ts` uses it on parsed tool-call payloads and nested `function` objects, and `selectedclips.ts` uses it while normalizing attachments, so preserving that import contract matters to four different call paths.",
      "occurrence_count": 2,
      "plan_ids": [
        "cleanup-and-quality-pass-on-20260404-0138"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-095",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: the current `commitdata()` and history pipeline treat each `applyedit()` as its own commit boundary. because step 16 proposes a post-commit reconcile edit after deletions, the actual callers here matter: without `skiphistory` or transaction coalescing, one user action will produce two history events and likely two saves.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "implement-two-tier-shot-20260406-1054"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-096",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: `handleassetdrop()` and the external-drop wrapper are not pure builders; they immediately mutate editor state and call `applyedit()` per asset. that means the plan cannot literally reuse those callers for atomic shot-drop creation without first extracting a lower-level helper, or it will violate its own \"single applyedit\" requirement.",
      "occurrence_count": 2,
      "plan_ids": [
        "implement-two-tier-shot-20260406-1054"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-111",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked the actual commit/history callers around step 18. `commitdata()` emits `beforecommit` and schedules save on every edit, and `usetimelinehistory.onbeforecommit()` pushes undo entries per commit boundary, so a reconcile-after-delete `applyedit` still fragments one user action into multiple history/save operations unless the plan also changes those caller contracts.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "implement-two-tier-shot-20260406-1054"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-115",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked the multi-drag callers in `useclipdrag`. the 'new track' grouped-drag branch still routes through `buildconfigfromdragresult()` and `applyedit({ type: 'config' })`, so the revised plan still does not fully handle every caller shape for grouped moves once pinned-group metadata becomes part of the timeline state.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "implement-two-tier-shot-20260406-1054"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-130",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked the `usepinnedgroupsync()` call site in `src/tools/video-editor/components/timelineeditor/timelineeditor.tsx:479` against the proposed context-based interaction guard. because sync is called directly from `timelineeditor`, routing its guard through new editor-context plumbing is not the most direct fit for the actual caller and depends on extra provider/type changes that the step does not enumerate.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "refactor-pinned-shot-groups-20260408-0508"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-138",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked the caller contract of `useselectedmediaclips()` via [usetimelineeditordata](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useselectedmediaclips.ts#l29). this hook's caller currently provides `selectedclipids` and `resolvedconfig`, not a resolved config object with `pinnedshotgroups`, so the plan still does not handle the actual arguments passed at that call site and needs to pull pinned groups from editor `data` instead.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "implement-first-class-shot-20260409-2052"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-158",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked the actual callers of the timeline-selection refs with `rg 'selectedclipidsref|additiveselectionref|selectedclipidref|selectedtrackidref' src/tools/video-editor`. save/poll paths consume `selectedclipidref` and `selectedtrackidref`, but `useclipdrag.ts`, `timelineeditor.tsx`, `videoeditorprovider.tsx`, and `usetimelinestate.contexts.ts` also consume `selectedclipidsref` and `additiveselectionref`, so the plan does not yet handle all caller shapes unless it adds a store-backed adapter for the multi-select refs as well.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "milestone-m1b-from-docs-state-20260416-1226"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-159",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked `setselectedclipid(` callers in `useassetmanagement.ts`, `usecliptextoverlay.ts`, `propertiespanel.tsx`, and `previewpanel.tsx`. those callers pass concrete clip ids and `null` through the existing dispatch-like api, so any store action that replaces `setselectedclipid` must preserve the same semantics for clearing and reselection rather than narrowing the contract to a simpler single-purpose setter.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "milestone-m1b-from-docs-state-20260416-1226"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-175",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked the real callers of the safe timeline hooks in `src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/useaddtovideoeditor.ts`. that code passes through both mounted-editor values and the `null` outside-provider case, then uses `ops.registergenerationasset`, `ops.handleassetdrop`, and `data.resolvedconfig?.clips` when available, so any replacement api has to preserve both nullable access and the current immediate-drop contract rather than only exposing non-null store selectors.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "milestone-m1c-from-docs-state-20260416-2053"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-176",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked `src/tools/video-editor/components/timelineeditor/timelinecanvas.tsx` as a caller of `usetimelineeditordata()`. it only reads `dataref`, but it passes that mutable ref straight into `useclipresizegesture`, which relies on latest timeline data during pointer sequences; the plan does not yet say how this exact caller will receive a stable mutable adapter once the context is removed.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "milestone-m1c-from-docs-state-20260416-2053"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-177",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked `src/tools/video-editor/components/videoeditorshell.tsx`, where `handlekeyboarddelete` combines `editordata.dataref.current` with `editordata.selectedclipids` to build a mutation before falling back to `handledeleteclips`. that caller needs synchronized latest-ref and latest-selection semantics, so splitting those reads across unrelated store selectors without an adapter can change behavior even if each field is individually correct.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "milestone-m1c-from-docs-state-20260416-2053"
      ]
    }

      "id": "DEBT-078",
      "concern": "search for related code that handles the same concept. is the reported issue a symptom of something broader?: checked the existing video-drop paths in `src/tools/video-editor/lib/external-drop-utils.ts:46-55,386-412` and `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useassetmanagement.ts:198-252`. the broader repository pattern for video assets is not just \u201cregister then insert\u201d; multi-generation video drops also patch `registry.duration` using `getdroppedgenerationduration()`, while `useshotfinalvideos()` in `src/tools/travel-between-images/hooks/video/useshotfinalvideos.ts:19-37` currently fetches no duration metadata. that means final-video replacement is broader than badge wiring alone if the new clip is expected to behave like other video assets in timing ui and resize logic.",

        "id": "DEBT-041",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the actual export surface in `supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/llm/messages.ts` and its consumers. step 6 says to 'remove local `isrecord`' from `messages.ts` and import it from `utils.ts`, but without also preserving `messages.ts` as an exporter or rewiring `index.ts`, `db.ts`, `tool-calls.ts`, and `selectedclips.ts`, that change would break the module graph immediately.",

        "id": "DEBT-055",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the render-gating cleanup plan against remotion's runtime exports in `node_modules/remotion/dist/cjs/cancel-render.js:46-70`. `cancelrender()` stores an error and throws to abort the render; it is not a benign cleanup for an abandoned `delayrender` handle, so step 2's instruction to call `cancelrender(handle)` on unmount is technically wrong and would abort preview or export with an error.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "add-audio-reactive-effects-to-20260406-0440"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-056",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked how remotion itself acquires delay handles in `node_modules/remotion/dist/cjs/iframe.js:8-18`. remotion's own components create the handle synchronously with `usestate(() => delayrender(...))`; the plan says 'calls delayrender() on mount', which reads like an effect-time action and leaves the initial-frame blocking behavior underspecified compared with the actual library pattern.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,

      {

        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the new `pinnedshotgroupsoverride` design is still underspecified against `materializedata()`. `assembletimelinedata()` computes `stablesignature` from the config it receives, so if the executor literally follows step 2.4 and overwrites `pinnedshotgroups` on an already-materialized `timelinedata` before `commitdata()`, the saved config and the cached `stablesignature` will diverge unless the data is rebuilt from the updated config.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "implement-two-tier-shot-20260406-1054"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-090",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the revised grouped-drag plan fixes the pointer-down selection regression, but it still leaves `pinnedshotgroup.trackid` stale on cross-track moves. the data model stores `trackid`, and current drag/move helpers only update rows/meta/clip order, so a pinned group dragged to another track would keep pointing at the old track unless the drag commit also patches group metadata.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "implement-two-tier-shot-20260406-1054"
        ]
      },
      {

        "plan_ids": [
          "implement-two-tier-shot-20260406-1054"
        ]
      },

        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: step 5.1 claims `updatepinnedgroupwithchildren` is 'currently imported by useswitchtofinalvideo.ts:357'. verified at useswitchtofinalvideo.ts:71-130 that this function is actually **defined locally** in useswitchtofinalvideo.ts, not imported from pinned-group-projection.ts. the real imports from pinned-group-projection at :5-8 are `buildgroupfromchildren` and `replacegroupchildren`, both of which are already in step 5's delete list. the plan's characterization is factually wrong (delete list shouldn't include updatepinnedgroupwithchildren as a pinned-group-projection export), but the substantive intent still works: once `buildgroupfromchildren`/`replacegroupchildren` are deleted, the local helper won't compile and step 11.2's 'inline soft-tag construction' rewrite removes it. minor wording error, not a blocking issue.",
